题目: _2DTo3D_N/easy/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_12-answ_D.png
Prompt类型: N类型
正确答案: D
预测答案: D
时间: 2025-07-23 01:23:36
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with different patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which matches the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
The only assembly that places the “spiky-star” circle (net A), the plain small-hexagon circle (net B), and the hexagon-frame face (net E) all in mutually consistent, fold-valid positions is choice D.  

So the correct answer is D.

使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with different patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which matches the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
The only cube whose three visible faces can be F4 (pentagon-X), F6 (circle-cross) and F5 (circle-prism) in mutually adjacent positions is choice A.  
Answer: A

使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with different patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which matches the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
The only folding that lets B–C and B–A lie on opposite sides of the top, D–E–F form the front–bottom–back band, and F end up as the front face is

Top = B, Front = F, Right = C.

Only option D shows on the top the hexagon B, on the front the triple-pentagon F, and on the right the pentagon+triangle C. 

Answer: D

题目: _2DTo3D_N/hard/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_29-answ_B.png
Prompt类型: N类型
正确答案: B
预测答案: B
时间: 2025-07-24 10:11:38
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with different patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which matches the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: B

Explanation (concise):  
Label the net faces as follows:

- Face C (center): pentagon with left-pointing wedge  
- Face 1 (above C): multi-sided polygon (heptagon)  
- Face 2 (left of C): circle with “X”  
- Face 3 (right of C): square border with circle and diamond  
- Face 4 (attached right of Face 3): circle border with right-pointing wedge  
- Face 5 (below Face 4): diamond in square  

When folded so that the pentagon (C) becomes the left face of the cube, its neighbors map to cube faces as  
• Face 1 → top  
• Face 3 → front  
• Face 4 → right  
• Face 2 → back  
• Face 5 → bottom  

Thus the three visible faces (top/front/right) should be: heptagon, square-circle-diamond, circle-wedge. Only option B shows exactly that arrangement.
==================================================

题目: _2DTo3D_N/hard/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_96-answ_B.png
Prompt类型: N类型
正确答案: B
预测答案: B
时间: 2025-07-24 13:52:25
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with different patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which matches the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: B

Explanation:

Label the six faces of the net as follows (using the same numbering you sketched):

• F1 (at net position r1,c1): small concentric hexagons  
• F2 (r2,c1): concentric circles with an inner pentagon  
• F3 (r2,c2): square border with the “#” grid inside  
• F4 (r2,c3): square border with the small diamond‐in‐a‐square inside  
• F5 (r2,c4): circle border with a left-pointing triangle  
• F6 (r3,c3): diamond border with the little cross-grid inside  

When you fold up the net:

– F3 becomes the front face  
– F4 folds around to become the right face  
– F6 folds underneath to become the bottom face  
– F2 becomes the left face  
– F1 ends up on top  
– F5 is hidden at the back  

Looking through the transparent net overlay in the answer choices, only choice B correctly places F3 on the front (you see the “#” grid boldly), F4 on one adjacent face, and F6 on the other adjacent face.  Choices A, C, and D either show the wrong face on the front or mix up the adjacencies.  Therefore the folded cube matches option B.
==================================================

题目: _2DTo3D_N/hard/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_109-answ_A.png
Prompt类型: N类型
正确答案: A
预测答案: A
时间: 2025-07-24 14:17:26
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with different patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which matches the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
1. Number the faces in the net as follows (row, col):
   • (1,1)=f₁₁: diamond–in–diamond  
   • (1,2)=f₁₂: pentagon–in–pentagon  
   • (2,2)=f₂₂: square–in–rotated-square (8-point star)  
   • (2,3)=f₂₃: heptagon–in–heptagon–in–triangle  
   • (3,2)=f₃₂: hexagon–in–pentagon–in–hexagon  
   • (3,3)=f₃₃: irregular pentagon–in–square–in–square  

2. Adjacencies by shared edges in the net:
   • f₂₂ neighbors f₁₂ (above), f₃₂ (below), f₂₃ (to the right)  
   • f₁₂ also neighbors f₁₁ (to the left)  
   • f₃₂ also neighbors f₃₃ (to the right)  

3. To choose the three‐visible‐faces on the folded cube, pick the triad sharing a common vertex in 3D. A natural choice is {f₁₂, f₂₂, f₃₂}, since in the net they meet around f₂₂.

4. In option A:
   • Top face = pentagon–in–pentagon = f₁₂  
   • Right face = square–in–rotated-square (8-point star) = f₂₂  
   • Front face = hexagon–in–pentagon–in–hexagon = f₃₂  

   All three match the net adjacencies (f₂₂ shares edges with f₁₂ above and f₃₂ below), and the orientations agree.

Therefore the correct folded‐cube view is option A.  

Answer：A
==================================================

题目: _2DTo3D_N/hard/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_120-answ_D.png
Prompt类型: N类型
正确答案: D
预测答案: X
时间: 2025-07-24 14:35:45
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with different patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which matches the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: X

Explanation:  
Label the six symbols as follows (matching the net):

• S1 = X-in-square (left net face)  
• S2 = “H”-shaped mark in a circle (center net face)  
• S3 = Pentagon (3rd net face)  
• S4 = Small circle in a square (4th net face)  
• S5 = Concentric circles with central diamond (top net face)  
• S6 = Diamond-in-diamond (bottom net face)  

From the net we have these adjacency and opposite‐face relations:

1. Adjacencies:  
   – S2 is adjacent to S1, S3, S5, S6.  
   – S3 is adjacent only to S2 and S4.  
   – S1 is adjacent only to S2.  
   – S4 is adjacent only to S3.  
   – S5 is adjacent only to S2.  
   – S6 is adjacent only to S2.  

2. Opposites:  
   – S2 opposite S4  
   – S1 opposite S3  
   – S5 opposite S6  

Checking each option:

A) Shows S5–S1 and S5–S3 as adjacent faces (invalid: S5 must only touch S2).  
B) Shows S1–S3 adjacent (invalid: S1 opposite S3).  
C) Shows S3–S5 adjacent (invalid: S3 only touches S2 and S4).  
D) Shows S2–S4 adjacent (invalid: S2 opposite S4).  

None of A–D can realize the net’s adjacency/opposite requirements. Therefore, the correct answer is X (none of the above).
==================================================

==================================================
题目: _2DTo3D_Y/easy/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_1-answ_C.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: C
预测答案: C
时间: 2025-07-24 15:33:23
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: C

Explanation:

Label the six faces in the net as follows:

  • F1 (row 1, col 1): “ring–square–diamond”  
  • F2 (row 2, col 1): hexagon with inner circle  
  • F3 (row 2, col 2): square+octagon border with a little triangle  
  • F4 (row 2, col 3): twisted‐square inside a pentagon  
  • F5 (row 2, col 4): pentagon with central diamond  
  • F6 (row 3, col 2): three concentric rings  

When you fold the net into a cube with F3 as the front face, you get:

  Front  = F3  
  Left   = F2  
  Right  = F4  
  Back   = F5  
  Top    = F1  
  Bottom = F6  

Check each candidate:

  A) All six patterns appear, and each adjacent pair in the drawing corresponds to two faces that are adjacent in the net.  
  B) ditto.  
  C) The cube shows F6 (the concentric rings) on the top face, but in the true folding F6 must go on the bottom.  This adjacency is impossible.  
  D) again matches the net adjacencies and orientations.

Hence the one that does NOT match is C.
==================================================

题目: _2DTo3D_Y/easy/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_12-answ_D.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: D
预测答案: B
时间: 2025-07-24 15:55:58
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: B

Explanation:
When the net is folded, the face with the circle‐in‐a‐square (F6) becomes the bottom face. The face with the irregular pentagon and inner shape (F1) becomes the top. Bottom (F6) and top (F1) are opposite faces on the cube and cannot share an edge; yet in option B they appear as adjacent faces (front vs. top), which is impossible. Options A, C, and D all place F6 adjacent only to lateral faces (F4, F5, or F3), which is consistent with the net. Therefore B is the one that does NOT match.
==================================================

预测答案: B

使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
The only “cube‐view” that can’t possibly come from this net is B. In B you see two faces (F₂ and F₆) that are opposite each other on the folded cube, so they could never both lie on adjacent faces of the model. Hence B does NOT match.

题目: _2DTo3D_Y/easy/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_17-answ_C.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: C
预测答案: C
时间: 2025-07-24 16:05:29
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
The only impossible pairing around the “diamond‐circle” face (P6) is having the pentagon‐diamond (P2) face adjacent to it. In option C the top face is P2, but P2 is opposite P6 on the cube, so C cannot occur.  
Answer: C

题目: _2DTo3D_Y/easy/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_45-answ_A.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: A
预测答案: D
时间: 2025-07-24 17:16:56
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: D

Explanation:

Number the net’s faces as follows (with coordinates in the unfolded layout):

       1   2   3
           4   5   6

Face 5 (the solid-filled diamond with corner-dots) sits at the front of the folded cube; in the net, its neighbors are
– above: face 3 (the concentric-circles/grid),
– right:  face 6 (the circle-“#”),
– left:   face 4 (the pentagon).

Thus, with face 5 fixed at front, the only valid placement of the remaining three faces on top/right/left is:

 top   = face 3    (concentric circles)
 right = face 6    (circle + hash)
 left  = face 4    (pentagon)

Only option D shows exactly those three patterns on the top, right, and front faces. A, B, and C place faces in positions that the net cannot yield, so they do not match.
==================================================

使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
The only impossible pairing in option A is that the “star” face and the “ring” face appear adjacent, yet in the net they are on opposite sides of the cube.  No rotation of the folded cube can make those two opposite faces share an edge.  Hence A does not match.  

Answer: A

==================================================
题目: _2DTo3D_Y/easy/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_54-answ_C.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: C
预测答案: D
时间: 2025-07-24 17:34:38
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: D

Explanation:

1.  Label the six faces in the net and their patterns as follows:  
   – A = circle ⃝ with inner pentagon  
   – B = tilted‐square “diamond” mosaic  
   – C = small pentagon with cross‐hatches  
   – D = concentric hexagons  
   – E = square frame with small hexagon inside  
   – F = large hexagon frame with inner filled triangle  

2.  From the net we see that the four in a row B–C–D–E fold into a band around the middle, with A folding up to become the face opposite F, and F folding down to become the face opposite A.  Thus the only allowed adjacent‐face pairs are:  
   • B–C, C–D, D–E, E–B  (the equatorial cycle)  
   • A adjacent to each of B, C, D, E  
   • F adjacent to each of B, C, D, E  
   All other pairs—namely A–F, B–D, and C–E—must lie on opposite faces and so cannot appear as neighbors on the folded cube.  

3.  In choice D, the circle‐pentagon face A and the triangle‐hexagon face F appear next to each other.  But in the net A and F are opposite faces, so they cannot be adjacent on the folded cube.  Choices A, B, and C all respect the allowed adjacencies, but D does not.  

Therefore the one that does NOT match the net is choice D.
==================================================

使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:

题目: _2DTo3D_Y/easy/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_68-answ_A.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: A
预测答案: D
时间: 2025-07-24 18:05:49
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: D

Explanation:
1. Label the six faces from the net:
   - P1 = concentric‐circles (net at row 1, col 1)
   - P2 = complex “cross‐diamond” (row 2, col 1)
   - P3 = hexagon‐star (row 2, col 2)
   - P4 = square‐mesh (row 2, col 3)
   - P5 = square‐border with diamond (row 2, col 4)
   - P6 = diamond‐border with circle (row 3, col 2)

2. When folded:
   - P2–P3–P4–P5 form the four‐face band around the cube.
   - P1 folds up to become one “pole” face (top or bottom).
   - P6 folds down to become the opposite “pole.”
   ⇒ P1 and P6 must be opposite faces (never adjacent on the cube).

3. Check the shown cubes (front, right, top are the three bold faces):
   - A, B, C each show at most one of P1 or P6 among those three faces.
   - D shows P1 on top and P6 on the right (two faces that should be opposite), which is impossible.

Therefore the cube in D cannot come from this net.
==================================================

题目: _2DTo3D_Y/easy/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_72-answ_D.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: D
预测答案: D
时间: 2025-07-24 18:14:56
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: D

Explanation:

Label the net faces as follows:

• F1 (top‐row left): square frame + small pentagon  
• F2 (top‐row middle): circle frame + small square  
• F3 (top‐row right): square frame + rotated pentagon  
• F4 (bottom‐row left under F2): hexagon frame + filled small hexagon  
• F5 (bottom‐row middle under F3): hexagon frame + “cross” inside  
• F6 (bottom‐row right): square frame + big “X”  

When folded so that F2 becomes the “front” face, its only opposite is F5; all the other faces (F1, F3, F4, F6) lie around F2. Thus none of those four can ever be opposite F2 or hidden directly behind it. In particular, if F2 is on the front, F5 cannot appear on any adjacent face (top/left/right/bottom) — it must lie on the back.

Option D shows F2 (the circle) on the front and F5 (the hexagon+cross) on the right (an adjacent face), which is impossible. A, B, and C all place only F1, F3, F4 or F6 next to F2, so they are valid. Hence the one that does NOT match the folded cube is D.
==================================================

题目: _2DTo3D_Y/easy/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_76-answ_C.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: C
预测答案: D
时间: 2025-07-24 18:22:48
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: D

Explanation (analysis of face adjacencies)

Label the six faces on the net as follows:

 • F1 = circle border with inner square  
 • F2 = pentagon border with inner diamond  
 • F3 = hexagon border with inner thick rectangle  
 • F4 = hexagon-border with “cage” (nested squares) interior  
 • F5 = small diamond border with arrow-heads interior  
 • F6 = pentagon border with five-point star interior  

A valid folding (one convenient choice) places:

 Front = F2  
 Left  = F1  
 Right = F3  
 Bottom= F6  
 Top   = F5  
 Back  = F4  

Any orientation of that cube simply rotates these six faces rigidly.  Hence whichever face you show “up front,” all and only its four true neighbors (from {F1,F2,F3,F5,F6}) must appear on the four adjoining faces.

Option D shows the cage-interior face (F4) up front, but one of its visible neighbors is the pentagon-with-diamond face (F2) – yet F2 is opposite F4 in the folded cube and so cannot lie on its side.  Thus D is impossible.  A, B and C, by contrast, each show a face up front together with exactly its four true neighbors, merely in different rotations.
==================================================

==================================================
题目: _2DTo3D_Y/easy/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_88-answ_B.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: B
预测答案: A
时间: 2025-07-24 18:43:30
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: A

Reasoning (using pattern‐adjacency constraints from the net):

Label the six face‐patterns by P1…P6 as follows:

P1 = circle with nested squares  
P2 = pentagon outline with filled diamond  
P3 = pentagon outline with inverted triangle  
P4 = diamond (45° square) outline with small pentagon  
P5 = hexagon outline with star  
P6 = square outline with small diamond  

From the unfolded net we deduce exactly which patterns touch on the cube:

– P4 (the “diamond outline → pentagon”) in the net is the central face, and it is adjacent only to P2, P3, and P5.  
– P2 is adjacent only to P4.  
– P3 is adjacent only to P4 and P1.  
– P5 is adjacent only to P4 and P6.  
– P1 is adjacent only to P3.  
– P6 is adjacent only to P5.

Thus the only allowed edge–adjacencies on the folded cube are:

  P4–P2, P4–P3, P4–P5, P3–P1, P5–P6  

Now inspect each answer cube’s three visible faces (front, top, right) and check their pairwise adjacencies:

(A) shows front = P2, top = P3, right = P1.  
    But front–top = P2–P3 is NOT an allowed adjacency.  
    ⇒ A is impossible.

(B) front = P2, right = P6, top = P1 ⇒ front–top = P2–P1 invalid.

(C) front = P4, right = P2, top = P1 ⇒ front–top = P4–P1 invalid.

(D) front = P5, right = P3, top = P4 ⇒ front–right = P5–P3 invalid.

Only one of these four must *not* match.  In fact A already violates the net’s adjacency (P2–P3), so X = A.
==================================================

题目: _2DTo3D_Y/easy/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_107-answ_C.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: C
预测答案: B
时间: 2025-07-24 19:33:08
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: B

Explanation:
1. Label the six net faces S1–S6 as follows:
   • S1: the nested‐pentagon/diamond face  
   • S2: the pentagon/triangle face  
   • S3: the square→circle→diamond face  
   • S4: the pentagon inscribed in a square face  
   • S5: the square→circle→hexagon face  
   • S6: the spiky “star” face  

2. When folded, the opposite‐face pairs are
   S1–S3, S2–S4, S5–S6.
   Any two opposite faces can never appear as adjacent faces on the cube.

3. Check which triple of faces appears on each 3-face view:
   A. shows S4 (front), S3 (right), S5 (top) → S4–S3, S4–S5, S3–S5 are all adjacent in the cube (OK)  
   B. shows S4 (front), S5 (right), S6 (top) → S5–S6 are opposite (cannot be adjacent) → IMPOSSIBLE  
   C. shows S4 (front), S6 (right), S3 (top) → S4–S6, S4–S3, S6–S3 are all adjacent (OK)  
   D. likewise shows an adjacent triple (OK)

Only option B attempts to display S5 and S6 on two faces that share an edge, which is impossible since S5 and S6 are opposite faces. Therefore B does NOT match the folded cube.
==================================================

题目: _2DTo3D_Y/easy/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_118-answ_D.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: D
预测答案: C
时间: 2025-07-24 19:54:16
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: C

Explanation:

Label the net’s faces as follows:

– F1 = leftmost hexagon (arrow-right)  
– F2 = center star  
– F3 = small diamond  
– F4 = rightmost hexagon (arrow-left)  
– F5 = top double-diamond  
– F6 = bottom “#”

When folded:

• Opposite pairs are (F1–F3), (F2–F4), (F5–F6).  
• Adjacent triples (corners) are exactly the sets  
 {F5,F2,F1}, {F5,F2,F3}, {F5,F4,F1}, {F5,F4,F3},  
 {F6,F2,F1}, {F6,F2,F3}, {F6,F4,F1}, {F6,F4,F3}.

Now inspect each option:

A shows the hexagon (arrow-right), the star, and the double-diamond meeting at a corner. Those are {F1, F2, F5}, which is valid.

B shows one hexagon, the star, and the “#” meeting at a corner. Those are {F3 or F1, F2, F6}, also valid.

C shows both hexagons and the small diamond meeting at one corner. That would be {F1, F3, F4}, but F1 and F3 are opposite faces—they cannot meet at a corner. Impossible.

D can be assigned to {F4, F2, F1} or another valid triple.

Thus C is the one that does NOT match.
==================================================

题目: _2DTo3D_Y/easy/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_137-answ_B.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: B
预测答案: A
时间: 2025-07-24 20:25:19
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
The only arrangement that forces the “star‐cross” face (P4) to sit next to the nested‐pentagon face (P2)—an adjacency that never exists in the net—is choice A. All the others can be folded so that P2 only touches its legal neighbors (P1, P3 and D). Therefore A is the one that does NOT match. Answer: A
==================================================

题目: _2DTo3D_Y/easy/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_143-answ_A.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: A
预测答案: A
时间: 2025-07-24 20:35:45
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: A. 

Explanation:

Label the six faces in the net as follows (using the order of attachment in the “snake” net F₁–F₂–F₃–F₄–F₅–F₆):

• F₁ (nested pentagon inside a square frame)  
• F₂ (circle boundary with a chevron/triangle)  
• F₃ (overlapping square + diamond “star” motif)  
• F₄ (pentagonal boundary with a chevron/triangle)  
• F₅ (pentagonal boundary with an inner circle)  
• F₆ (nested pentagon‐within‐pentagon)

When folded, the opposite‐face pairs are (F₁∥F₆), (F₂∥F₄), (F₃∥F₅).  No two members of any of these three pairs can appear on adjacent faces of the cube.

In option A, the two nested‐pentagon faces (F₁ and F₆)—which must be opposite—are shown adjacent (top face vs. front face).  That is impossible, so A cannot match the net.  Options B, C and D all respect the required opposite‐face relationships and are valid rotations of the folded cube, but A violates them.
==================================================

==================================================
题目: _2DTo3D_Y/hard/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_1-answ_D.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: D
预测答案: C
时间: 2025-07-24 20:46:45
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: C

Explanation:
Label the six faces in the net as follows:

  • U (top of net, row 1 col 2): the “star around a circle”  
  • F (middle row col 2): the square‐border-with-diamond  
  • D (bottom of net, row 3 col 2): the irregular pentagon with inner squares  
  • L (middle row col 1): the concentric circle-with-two-hexagons  
  • R (middle row col 3): the hexagon-with-four-spokes  
  • B (middle row col 4): the circle-with-hexagon-border-and-diamond  

When you fold the net, any three faces that meet at one corner can be brought into view by a rigid rotation of the cube.  Checking each option:

  A shows L → front, F → top, D → right.  
  B shows  F → front, U → top, R → right.  
  C shows D → front, F → top, R → right.  
  D shows U → front, L → top, B → right.  

All four of those triples (L,F,D), (F,U,R), (D,F,R), and (U,L,B) are indeed mutually adjacent on the cube – so each could in principle be obtained by some orientation of the folded cube.  However, only in choice C does the pentagon‐face D appear with the wrong rotational alignment relative to its neighbors F and R.  No rigid rotation of the cube can carry the bottom face (D) into front position with exactly that orientation of its pattern.  All the other three options preserve each face’s correct orientation.  

Hence C is the one that cannot match the folded net.
==================================================

题目: _2DTo3D_Y/hard/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_49-answ_B.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: B
预测答案: B

使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: B

Explanation: Numbering the net faces by their distinct patterns gives:

1. The irregular pentagon with the four small circles  
2. The square‐frame with the hexagon  
3. The pentagon‐frame with the internal grid  
4. The concentric circle–hexagon–circle  
5. The square‐frame with the circle  
6. The square‐frame with the pentagon  

When this net is folded into a cube, opposites are (1–4), (2–6), (3–5), and every face is adjacent to all but its opposite.  A valid “three‐face” view must show three mutually adjacent faces.  Checking each option:

A shows faces {3, 4, 6} – all three are pairwise adjacent.  
B shows faces {1, 4, 5} – but 1 and 4 are opposites, so cannot meet at one corner.  
C shows faces {2, 3, 4} – all adjacent.  
D shows faces {1, 2, 5} – all adjacent.

Only B attempts to display two opposite faces together, so B does NOT match the folded cube.

题目: _2DTo3D_Y/hard/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_58-answ_C.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: C
预测答案: B
时间: 2025-07-24 23:07:19
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: B

Explanation:

Label the six net‐faces as follows (with respect to the unfolded layout):

• P2 = the “big hexagon + central circle” face  
• P1 = small single‐outline pentagon (left of P2)  
• P3 = plain single‐outline hexagon (right of P2)  
• P4 = the star‐shaped circle face (far right)  
• P5 = the nested‐pentagon face (above P2)  
• P6 = the rotated‐pentagon face (below P3)

When you fold up the net:

– Front (F) = P2  
– Left  (L) = P1  
– Right (R) = P3  
– Back  (B) = P4  
– Up    (U) = P5  
– Down  (D) = P6  

Every valid corner of the folded cube must be a triple of faces that actually meet at one vertex.  In particular:

– F, L, D  =  P2–P1–P6 is a genuine corner  
– F, R, U  =  P2–P3–P5  
– R, U, B  =  P3–P5–P4  
– L, U, B  =  P1–P5–P4  
– ... etc.

Option B is the only one that shows P4 (the star face) adjacent to P2 (the big‐circle face).  But in the true folding P4 (back) is opposite P2 (front) and can never share an edge or corner with it.  That makes B impossible.
==================================================

题目: _2DTo3D_Y/hard/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_68-answ_B.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: B
预测答案: D
时间: 2025-07-24 23:41:22
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Let the six faces of the net be numbered (as in the unfolding) 1 through 6:  

- Face 1: the small hexagon‐with‐cross  
- Face 2: the “cube”-/hexagon-shape  
- Face 3: the concentric-circles with tiny pentagon  
- Face 4: the circle with big “X”  
- Face 5: the three nested pentagons  
- Face 6: the circle with inner diamond  

When folded, the opposite‐face pairs are (1–3), (2–4), (5–6), and each corner of the cube corresponds to one of these eight triples of mutually adjacent faces:

Top–front–right:
  {1,2,5}, {2,3,5}, {3,4,5}, {4,1,5}

Bottom–front–right:
  {1,2,6}, {2,3,6}, {3,4,6}, {4,1,6}

Now inspect the three faces shown in each option:

A shows faces {1,3,6} – but 1 and 3 are opposites, so they can never share an edge → impossible.  
B shows {2,4,5} – but 2 and 4 are opposites → impossible.  
C shows {1,2,3} – but 1 and 3 are opposites → impossible.  
D shows {1,2,5} – this is one of the legal corner‐triples → possible.  

Hence D is the only view that *can* come from that net; A, B and C all show forbidden opposite‐face adjacencies.  The one that does *not* match the folded cube is therefore any of A, B, C – but only D is a valid view.  Since the question asks for the *single* impossible one, the correct choice is  

D
==================================================

题目: _2DTo3D_Y/hard/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_72-answ_A.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: A
预测答案: D
时间: 2025-07-24 23:47:29
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: D

Explanation:

Label the six net‐faces as follows (with F₄ the central hexagon-with-nested-pentagon face):

  • F₁ (triangle in pentagon)  
  • F₂ (diamond in pentagon)  
  • F₃ (circle in pentagon)  
  • F₄ (hexagon with two nested polygons)  
  • F₅ (small diamond in diamond)  
  • F₆ (small square in square)  

In the net F₄ is surrounded (left, right, top, bottom) by F₃, F₅, F₂, F₆, and F₁ is attached only to F₃.  Thus when folded F₁ is on the face opposite F₄ and must never appear adjacent to it.

Option D shows the triangle-in-pentagon face (F₁) directly touching the hexagon-with-nested-pentagon face (F₄), which is impossible.  Hence D is the one that does *not* match.
==================================================

题目: _2DTo3D_Y/hard/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_85-answ_C.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: C
预测答案: B
时间: 2025-07-25 00:15:41
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: B

Explanation:

Label the six faces in the net as follows:

  • Face 2 (the “front” of the net) = regular hexagon with a square inside  
  • Face 1 (to its left) = irregular pentagon with a small filled pentagon inside  
  • Face 3 (to its right) = regular hexagon with the starburst inside  
  • Face 4 (to the right of Face 3 in the net) = diamond (rotated square) with a triangle inside  
  • Face 5 (above Face 2) = two concentric circles with a square inside  
  • Face 6 (below Face 2) = irregular pentagon with a triangle inside  

When folded:

  – Face 2 becomes the front face of the cube.  
  – Face 4 (which is attached only to Face 3 in the net) wraps around to become the back face, opposite Face 2.  

Thus Faces 2 and 4 end up opposite each other on the folded cube and cannot both appear on adjacent “visible” faces of any single corner.

Option B, however, shows Face 2 (hexagon with square) on the front and Face 4 (diamond with triangle) on a side face.  That is impossible, since those two faces are opposite on the actual cube.  Options A, C, and D each only display faces that are edge-adjacent in the folded cube.
==================================================

预测答案: B
时间: 2025-07-25 00:17:26
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
The only net‐fold that ever puts an opposite pair (here the “X‐in‐square” face and the “triangle‐in‐circle‐in‐hexagon” face) next to each other is B. All of A, C and D respect the three forbidden opposite adjacencies, but B shows F2 and F4 sharing an edge, so B cannot come from this net.  

Answer: B

使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Here’s the quick check using the fact that on the assembled cube the only forbidden adjacencies are the three opposite‐face pairs from the net:

– Opposites are (F2-F6), (F3-F5) and (F1-F4).  
– In option B the two bold faces “square + pentagon” (F5) and “circle + pentagon” (F3) appear side by side, yet F3 and F5 are opposite in the net and thus can’t share an edge when folded.  

Therefore B is impossible.  

Answer: B

题目: _2DTo3D_Y/hard/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_116-answ_D.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: D
预测答案: A
时间: 2025-07-25 01:56:29
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: A

Explanation:

Number the unfolded‐net faces as follows:

  • F1 at (0,0): concentric pentagons + circle  
  • F2 at (1,0): overlapping rotated squares (diamond)  
  • F3 at (1,1): diamond with small filled polygon  
  • F4 at (2,1): “cube” (hexagon)  
  • F5 at (1,2): pentagon + inner diamond  
  • F6 at (2,2): pentagon + inner triangle  

When folded into a cube:

  – F3 (1,1) becomes the bottom face, F6 (2,2) the top face.  
  – F2 is the back, F5 the front, F4 the right, F1 the left.  
  – Opposite pairs: (F1–F4), (F2–F5), (F3–F6).  

A correct cube‐view must show three faces that all meet at one vertex.  Those 8 possible triples are

  (front, right, top):   (F5,F4,F6)  
  (right, back, top):    (F4,F2,F6)  
  (back, left, top):     (F2,F1,F6)  
  (left, front, top):    (F1,F5,F6)  
  (front, right, bottom):(F5,F4,F3)  
  (right, back, bottom): (F4,F2,F3)  
  (back, left, bottom):  (F2,F1,F3)  
  (left, front, bottom): (F1,F5,F3)  

In option A the three visible faces are patterns F2 (diamond of squares), F3 (diamond+filled), and F5 (pentagon+diamond).  But F2 and F5 are opposite faces, so they cannot both meet F3 at one vertex.  Hence A cannot arise from the given net, whereas B, C, and D each display a valid triple.
==================================================

使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
The one impossible configuration is A. In A you see both the “bottom” face (the pentagon‐inside‐pentagon‐plus‐triangle) and the “left” face (the diamond‐with‐dot), but in the net those two faces are opposite and so can never lie adjacent as shown. A is therefore the only view that cannot come from the given unfolding.

使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: D

Explanation: In option D the visible faces are  
- f1 (outer circle, inner pentagon),  
- f2 (outer circle, inner square),  
- f3 (outer hexagon, inner circle).  

On the net, f1 is only attached to f2, and f3 is only attached to f2 (and f6). f1 and f3 are never edge‐adjacent when the net is folded, so they cannot appear sharing an edge (top/front) as in D. All other options only show faces that are adjacent in the folded cube.

题目: _2DTo3D_Y/hard/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_128-answ_B.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: B
预测答案: B
时间: 2025-07-25 02:22:05
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: B

Reasoning (using the standard labeling from the net):

1. Label the six faces from the net as follows when the cube is folded:
   • H = the nested hexagon (net center)  
   • P = the nested pentagon (net top‐right)  
   • D = the diamond/rhombus (net middle‐left)  
   • S = the square-in-star (net middle-right)  
   • C = the circle-around-diamond (net bottom)  
   • T = the 8-point star (net top‐left)  

2. On the folded cube these sit:
   • Front = H  
   • Back = T  (H ⟷ T are opposite)  
   • Left = D  
   • Right = S  (D ⟷ S are opposite)  
   • Top = P  
   • Bottom = C  (P ⟷ C are opposite)  

3. A valid 3-face view must be three faces that meet at a single vertex.  The only such triples on this cube are:
   H–P–S, H–S–C, H–C–D, H–D–P,  T–S–P, T–P–D, T–D–C, T–C–S.

4. In option B the three visible bold faces are
   • Front = T  
   • Top   = H  
   • Right = D  
   i.e. the triple T–H–D.  But H and T are opposite faces in the real cube and so cannot appear as adjacent faces in the same view.  Therefore B is impossible, and does NOT match the given net. 

Options A, C and D all show triples that do occur at a single vertex of the folded cube. Hence the correct answer is B.
==================================================

Brief Explanation: In the unfolded net the face B2 (the one with the small filled triangle) is opposite the face B4 (the one with the small diamond). In option C those two appear on adjacent sides of the cube (front and right), which is impossible. Options A, B and D all respect the “opposite‐face” relationships from the net, so C is the one that does NOT match.

题目: _2DTo3D_Y/hard/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_131-answ_B.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: B
预测答案: D
时间: 2025-07-25 02:28:26
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: D

Explanation:  
Label the six net‐faces by their central blue motif:

1. Diamond+four arrows  
2. Hexagon+diamond  
3. Hexagon–hexagon–circle–triangle  
4. Square with two concentric circles  
5. Pentagon+square  
6. Star of overlapping hexagons  

When folded, opposite pairs are (1↔6), (2↔5), (3↔4).  Any valid folding must never place one of those opposite‐pair motifs on two faces that share an edge.

Checking the four candidate cubes:

A shows faces 5, 3, 1 together (no forbidden opposite pair adjacent) → OK  
B shows faces 2, 4, 1 together → OK  
C shows faces 6, 4, 5 together → OK  
D shows faces 3 and 4 adjacent, but 3↔4 are opposite in the net → INVALID  

Hence D cannot arise from folding the given net.
==================================================

题目: _2DTo3D_Y/hard/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_133-answ_C.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: C
预测答案: C
时间: 2025-07-25 02:33:38
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: C

Reasoning:  
Label the six patterns as follows:  
• P1 = square border with pentagon  
• P2 = nested polygons  
• P3 = circle with diamond  
• P4 = circle with triangular “propeller”  
• P5 = hexagon border with little squares  
• P6 = simple circle‐ring  

From the net one finds the opposite‐face pairs must be  
 P1–P6, P2–P4, P3–P5  
and the adjacency sets  
 P1 adjacent to {P2,P3,P4,P5}  
 P2 adjacent to {P1,P3,P5,P6}  
 P3 adjacent to {P1,P2,P4,P6}  
 P4 adjacent to {P1,P3,P5,P6}  
 P5 adjacent to {P1,P2,P4,P6}  
 P6 adjacent to {P2,P3,P4,P5}  

Check each option by its three visible faces (front, right, top):

A. (front=P3, right=P6, top=P4)  
 P3–P6 and P3–P4 and P6–P4 all allowed by the above.

B. (front=P2, right=P5, top=P6)  
 P2–P5, P2–P6, P5–P6 all allowed.

C. (front=P4, right=P6, top=P2)  
 P4–P6 is allowed, but P4–P2 is not in P4’s adjacency list → Impossible.

D. (front=P6, right=P5, top=P4)  
 P6–P5, P6–P4, P5–P4 all allowed.

Only option C places P2 and P4 adjacent, yet they must be opposite. So C does NOT match.
==================================================

题目: _2DTo3D_Y/hard/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_137-answ_C.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: C
预测答案: A
时间: 2025-07-25 02:48:41
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: A

Explanation:

Label the five patterned faces in the unfolded net as follows:
• F (center face): the star‐like shape  
• U (top): circle‐border with inner diamond  
• R (right): square‐border with inner pentagon  
• D (bottom): square‐border with inner circle  
• L (left): the nested‐polygon “heptagon” shape  

In the cube‐fold, only triples of faces that form a 3‐clique in the net’s adjacency graph can appear together on the visible “front–top–right” of a folded cube.  The only triangles in that graph are

 (F,U,R), (F,R,D), (F,D,L), (F,L,U).

Each valid visible triple must include F (the center) together with two adjacent rim faces.

Option A shows the triple (L, U, R)—none of which is F—so it cannot arise from that net.  Hence A is the one that does NOT match.

使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: D

Why D is wrong:
- In the net, the small pentagon-with-triangle face (call it P₂, net face 2) is opposite the pentagon-with-circle face (P₄, net face 4).  Opposite faces on a cube can never share an edge.
- In option D, those two patterns appear on adjacent faces (front and right), which is impossible.

Options A, B, and C all respect the net’s adjacency/opposite relationships.

题目: _2DTo3D_Y/hard/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_145-answ_C.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: C
预测答案: D
时间: 2025-07-25 03:05:47
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Question Type: Paper-fold cube matching puzzle

Key Observations:
1. Label the net faces (row 2, columns 1–4; row 1, col 2; row 3, col 2) as M1–M4, U, D.
2. Opposite pairs from the net: M1–M3, M2–M4, U–D.
3. A valid folded cube cannot show an opposite pair on two adjacent faces.

Analysis of Options:
- A shows faces M3 (front), D (top), M4 (right). None of these are opposite in the net.
- B shows M2 (front), U (top), M3 (right). None are opposite in the net.
- C shows M1 (front), M4 (top), D (right). None are opposite in the net.
- D shows M4 (front) adjacent to M2 (top), but M2–M4 are opposite in the net, so they cannot share an edge.

Answer: D
==================================================

题目: _2DTo3D_Y/hard/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_146-answ_A.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: A
预测答案: C
时间: 2025-07-25 03:07:09
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
The net’s opposite‐face pairs are  
  • P1 (circle+pentagon) ⟷ P6 (square+pentagon/triangle)  
  • P2 (circle+asterisk) ⟷ P4 (diamond ×)  
  • P3 (diamond+circle) ⟷ P5 (square+X)  

Any valid “corner view” can only show three mutually adjacent faces, never an opposite pair.  
Option C’s three visible faces are P1 and P6 (opposite) together with P2, which is impossible.  

Answer: C

题目: _2DTo3D_Y/hard/_2DimgType-Symmetric-sample_149-answ_B.png
Prompt类型: Y类型
正确答案: B
预测答案: D
时间: 2025-07-25 03:13:43
使用的Prompt: You are given a paper fold puzzle, which is an unfolded cube with distinct patterns on each face. Please select the correct option (marked as A, B, C or D) at the bottom, which does NOT match the folded cube of this unfolded pattern. Answer：X
大模型完整回复:
Answer: D

Reasoning:

Label the six net‐faces as follows (with their shorthand codes):

  • F1 = circle+triangle  
  • F2 = pentagon+cross  
  • F3 = pentagon+inner-hexagon  
  • F4 = simple hexagon  
  • F5 = hexagon+gear  
  • F6 = square+cross  

From the net (and one consistent folding) you find the opposite pairs on the cube are

  • F1 ↔ F6  
  • F2 ↔ F4  
  • F3 ↔ F5  

Every other pair of faces on the cube shares an edge (i.e. is adjacent).

Now inspect the three bolded (front, right, top) faces in each option:

  A. {F6, F5, F4} – no pair is one of the opposite pairs ⇒ all three can meet at a corner ⇒ valid  
  B. {F1, F3, F2} – none of these are opposite ⇒ valid  
  C. {F6, F5, F4} – same set as A up to permutation ⇒ valid  
  D. {F1, F3, F5} – F3 and F5 are an opposite pair ⇒ cannot be adjacent ⇒ invalid  

Thus the only cube that does ​NOT​ match the folded net is D.
